# Ashe's sound effects (and B.O.B.'s): <sound id> = <file under E:/Overwatch Archive/extracted/154088/> (build 154088). Same
# format as illari_sfx.txt; her voice is ashe.txt (ashe_voice_gen.py). build_asset_pack.py sounds copies / mixes them
#  into the local pack.
# Read 2026-11-14 offline (Overwatch and Battle.net checked closed before every archive read and DataTool / TankLib run):
# her 02C sounds' Wwise banks decoded (ww.py, dump_banks.py), the Init bank's game parameter defaults (1E818, stmg.py --offline).
# Scripts: E:/Overwatch Archive/research/ashe/scripts/sounds/ (ashe_gen.py writes this file from ashe_table.py; Illari's
# pipeline copied and retargeted: fx_graph_ashe.py, fx_sounds2_ashe.py, clip_sounds_ashe.py, contacts.py, ww.py,
# dump_banks.py, extract_banks.py, measure_ashe.py, overview.py, stmg.py, ashe_events.py, ashe_layers.py, peek_layers.py);
# their data: research/ashe/sounds/ (ashe_ww.json, banks_raw.json, ashe_measure.json, contacts.json, overview.txt,
# clip_sounds.txt, clip_names.txt, ashe_ids.txt) and research/ashe/work/ (graph_effects.json, fx_sounds.txt,
# fx_sounds_timed.json). Her skin extraction is research/ashe/extract and the 45 banks it lacked (the shots' pass-by slots and
# contacts, the pellets', the dynamite's and B.O.B.'s contacts, his shot slot, immobilize, targeting, the perks') were
# extracted into research/ashe/sounds/ExtraSounds/Ashe (extract_banks.py; groups Viper, Coach, Dynamite, Bob).
# Hip and scoped shots are different effects (280E's 2458 / 29A1's 25E5), so no RTPC split is needed. The scoped shot's lever
# (2445 / the Viper's 1P clip) has no 3P copy. 66FF / 6891 switch on group 3526866566 (8 cases, read at its default, L what).
# B.O.B. has no voice set lines (01CD.05F is empty); his sounds are effects here, his no-range copies read as Ashe's
#  (the owner's).
# No sound at these moments (H by absence in their nodes, effects and clips): B.O.B.'s despawn (1537 #375 / #388, clips 604E /
# 6025 / 604D) and his drop (spawn_drop 5FE3); the dynamite in hand (2B66's child 3028 -> 2B22); the fuse lighting on landing
# (1502 #180: 2703 / 25C8 / 2625 name no sound); the Coach Gun's self push.
# Not mapped: 0186 / 0FCB (the shared ability-denied); Stadium / skin sets (6558 / 6556 / 5F58 / 5FE8 / 435C 858D / 7449 F51A /
# 744A F519 / 6450 C257 C25E 0556 0558 / 65B4 C3C5 C3C6 Airburst / 450E 8E51 Viper's Sting, extracted, L when); 013A 10C9 (14FC
# #587, scoped and 1 < v174 < 10, L) and 0231 164E (#591 after #606 0.08 s, L); 2717 4EFF / 4F0C (1537 #523 every 0.8-1.6 s,
# L what), 2718 4DFE (#528, L), 020D 1622 / 1623 (#213, shared), 2684 4DC1 (153A targeting, L); B.O.B.'s other clips (aim
# 4DEE, react 4F4F, base 5E3A / 4EEE-4DF3, a43c 4EDE / 4DF1, a47e 4EE7, knockdown 4DF5 / 4CB1 / 4EFB, uppercut_end 4F4D); her
# knockdown / emote / select / intro / highlight clips; the hero contact set 0665 (footsteps) and the Viper's 0018; the other
# 22 world materials and the barrier pair 1833 / 1834 (no files); 004B / 0033 / 01C7 / 01CF shared effects.
# Ids: 02C sounds (5D50 = 000000005D50.02C), 00D effects, 006 clips, 08F clip animation effects, 049 contact sets; graphs
# 01B with their node numbers. Confidence: H read in the data, M inferred, L guess. Stadium powers' sets are left out.
# Gains: Wwise volume + make-up gain summed from each file up through its containers and mixers (the middle of a random
# range included) plus its output bus chain, plus RTPC curves (dB scaling 20*log10(1+y); linear between points) and
# SetVolume actions. RTPCs are read at their game parameter's default (the Init bank's list); modulators: an LFO at its
# middle, a time modulator at its start on one-shots and at its end on loops. As in sojourn.txt, ONE offset per family keeps
# her sounds' balance with each other, bringing the family's loudest single layer to -1 dBFS (the builder's limiter catches
# sums): own -7.5 dB (loudest: ashe.dynamite.explode), 3p -3 dB (loudest: ashe.coach_gun.hit_3p).
# Pitch: cents as a speed factor (*). Low / high-pass filters, random pitch / volume per play, fades, Seek, Wwise states
# and the voice priority RTPCs are not reproduced. Layers more than 60 dB down are left out, and in loops those 20 dB
# under the loudest loop. A loop's LOOP LENGTH is its longest bed within 6 dB of the loudest layer; shorter layers are
# tiled across it and beds more than 15 % longer are left out.
# Own vs others: audience-1 copies and no-range copies are the player the effect plays on ('own': Ashe, the shooter for
# a hit, the burning one for the burn, the knocked-up one for immobilize, Ashe for B.O.B.'s, M as in Illari's map);
#  ranged copies are everyone else's ('3p'); where two ranged copies hold the same files the
# louder is built (L which team's / near or far). A sound with no own copy is built once for everyone ('both').

# ===== How each game event is put together (rules)
# ashe.viper.fire: The Viper hip shot, own: 66FF (4 of 2.58-3.28 s and 4 of 2.72-3.48 s tails, two 0.14 s cracks).
# Trigger: PRIMARY unscoped (14FC #767 -> volley #770, the hip shot 280E): 280E's STUEffectComponent 2456 ->
#  sub-effect 2458, H; 66FF (no range, audience 1: hers).
# Layers: a 66FF 573342763 RanSeq 2.58-3.28 s x4, net -5; b 66FF 834706602 RanSeq 2.72-3.48 s x4, net -10.5; c 66FF
#  949222550 Sound 0.14 s, net -16; d 66FF 205333548 RanSeq 0.14 s -800 cents, net -12.
# Left out: 66FF 438810987 (-208 dB), 66FF 644234063 (-208.5 dB).
@ashe.viper.fire = ashe.viper.fire.a@0.02:-12.5 + ashe.viper.fire.b@0.085:-18 + ashe.viper.fire.c@0.03:-23.5 + ashe.viper.fire.d*0.630@0.06:-19.5
# ashe.viper.fire_3p: The Viper hip shot heard by others: 6891 (200 m: 5 of 0.37-0.39 s cracks and 2.6-3.9 s tails).

#  sub-effect 2458, H; 6891 (200 m; C255 the same sets, the first built).

#  RanSeq 2.72-3.48 s x4, net -22.5.

#  after a scoped shot only (H).

# ashe.coach_gun.fire: Coach Gun blast, own: 5E3F (a 0.60 s blast, 3.6 s tails) + 4F7C + 8DE7 + the gun's handling
#  (4DFD, 4DFC at 0.4 s).
# Trigger: ABILITY 1 (1513 StateAbility #84 -> #107 Wait 0.55 s, blast at #109 0.165 s), H: #177 275C's 5E3F (audience
#  1: hers; its 0.60 s blast at 0.23 s) + #178 2764's 4F7C (audience 1; M that #448 is false in a normal game) + #139
#  2455's 8DE7 at 0.165 s + her 1P clip coach_gun 5AA9's effect 2A44: 4DFD @0, 4DFC @0.4 (no range; its 4F5D is -102

# ===== For the integration: each event, loop or one-shot, who it is built for, its length, and what triggers it
# (own = the player the effect plays on, in the head: Ashe, the shooter, the burning / knocked-up one; 3p = everyone else, at her
# / the dynamite / B.O.B. / the target; both = one rule for everyone). 'EXTRA' = in the data but not needed for the
# kit's moments. Not built: see 'Not mapped'.
#   ashe.viper.fire                    one-shot own  3.57 s; PRIMARY unscoped (14FC #767 -> volley #770, the hip shot
#     280E): 280E's STUEffectComponent 2456 -> sub-effect 2458, H; 66FF (no range, audience 1: hers)
#   ashe.viper.fire_3p                 one-shot 3p   3.99 s; PRIMARY unscoped (14FC #767 -> volley #770, the hip shot
#     280E): 280E's STUEffectComponent 2456 -> sub-effect 2458, H; 6891 (200 m; C255 the same sets, the first built)
#   ashe.viper.fire_ads                one-shot own  3.76 s; PRIMARY scoped (14FC #549 -> volley #555, the scoped shot
#     29A1): 29A1's STUEffectComponent 25E4 -> sub-effect 25E5, H; 66FE (no range, audience 1: hers) + the lever: the
#     Viper's 1P clip add_ads_fire_1p 5E8E's effect 2AB8: 4C8B @0.267 (no range) and 14FC #773 -> #666 Wait 0.3 s ->
#     #692 ActionEffect 2445: 63ED (audience 1), after a scoped shot only (H)
#   ashe.viper.fire_ads_3p             one-shot 3p   3.76 s; PRIMARY scoped (14FC #549 -> volley #555, the scoped shot
#     29A1): 29A1's STUEffectComponent 25E4 -> sub-effect 25E5, H; 6892 (200 m; C256 the same sets, the first built).
#     The lever has no 3P copy (4C8C, 32 m, is named only by the 1P clip)
#   ashe.viper.whiz (EXTRA)            one-shot both 1.97 s; each hip shot: 280E's m_2170A015 'sound only' slot effect
